---- CMakeLists (samp) ----

---- FAYL: app/src/main/cpp/CMakeLists.txt  (22 sətir, ilk 120 göstərilir) ----
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.12)
     2	
     3	project(CaspianProject LANGUAGES C CXX)
     4	
     5	# Harici bağımlılıklar
     6	find_package(shadowhook REQUIRED CONFIG)
     7	
     8	# Alt dizinler
     9	add_subdirectory(opus)
    10	add_subdirectory(samp)
    11	
    12	# 1. Global include_directories yerine, Opus veya Samp'in ihtiyacına göre hedef bazlı ekleme yapın
    13	# (Genellikle Opus kütüphanesinin kendi CMakeLists.txt dosyasıinclude dizinlerini zaten ayarlar)
    14	target_include_directories(opus PRIVATE
    15	    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/opus/include
    16	)
    17	
    18	# 2. Optimizasyon bayrağı (Release modunda CMake zaten -O2 / -O3 uygular ancak manuel eklemek güvenlidir)
    19	target_compile_options(opus PRIVATE -O3)
    20	
    21	# 3. Eğer samp kütüphanesi shadowhook kullanıyorsa, burada linklenmesi gerekebilir:
    22	# target_link_libraries(samp PRIVATE shadowhook::shadowhook)

375:         static inline float Smooth(float cur, float target, float dt, float speed) { return Lerp(cur, target, 1.0f - expf(-dt * speed)); }
376:         static inline float EaseOutCubic(float t) { const float p = 1.0f - t; return 1.0f - p * p * p; }

399:         {
400:                 if (id < 0 || id >= 96) return 0.0f;
401:                 float& p = s_prs[id];
402:                 p = Lerp(p, (s_act == id && g_mmTouch.down) ? 1.0f : 0.0f, 1.0f - expf(-dt * 24.0f));
403:                 return p;

407:         static bool Tap(int id, const Rc& r, bool canInput)
408:         {
409:                 if (!canInput) return false;
410:                 if (g_mmTouch.pressed && s_act == 0 && In(r, g_mmTouch.x, g_mmTouch.y)) s_act = id;
411:                 if (g_mmTouch.released && s_act == id) return In(r, g_mmTouch.x, g_mmTouch.y);
412:                 return false;
413:         }

429:         // bir slider satiri; deyer deyisibse true
430:         static bool SliderRow(int idx, const Rc& row, const char* label, float* v, float mn, float mx, float dt, bool canInput, const char* unit = "%", float step = 5.0f)
431:         {
432:                 const int id = ID_SLIDER + idx * 4;
433:                 bool changed = false;
434: 
435:                 const float cy = row.y + S(47.0f);
436:                 const Rc minus{ row.x + S(14.0f), cy - S(15.0f), S(30.0f), S(30.0f) };
437:                 const Rc plus { row.x + row.w - S(44.0f), cy - S(15.0f), S(30.0f), S(30.0f) };
438:                 const float tx0 = row.x + S(60.0f), tx1 = row.x + row.w - S(60.0f);
439:                 const Rc hit{ tx0 - S(16.0f), row.y + S(22.0f), (tx1 - tx0) + S(32.0f), row.h - S(22.0f) };
440: 
441:                 if (canInput)
442:                 {
443:                         const MMTouch& t = g_mmTouch;
444:                         if (t.pressed && s_act == 0) {
445:                                 if (In(minus, t.x, t.y))      s_act = id + 1;
446:                                 else if (In(plus, t.x, t.y))  s_act = id + 2;
447:                                 else if (In(hit, t.x, t.y))   s_act = id;
448:                         }
449:                         if (s_act == id && t.down) {
450:                                 const float f  = Clamp((t.x - tx0) / (tx1 - tx0), 0.0f, 1.0f);
451:                                 const float nv = floorf(mn + f * (mx - mn) + 0.5f);
452:                                 if (nv != *v) { *v = nv; changed = true; }
453:                         }
454:                         if (t.released) {
455:                                 float nv = *v;
456:                                 if (s_act == id + 1 && In(minus, t.x, t.y)) nv = Clamp(*v - step, mn, mx);
457:                                 if (s_act == id + 2 && In(plus,  t.x, t.y)) nv = Clamp(*v + step, mn, mx);
458:                                 if (nv != *v) { *v = nv; changed = true; }
459:                         }
460:                 }
461: 
462:                 const bool dragging = (s_act == id && g_mmTouch.down);
463:                 const float target = Clamp((*v - mn) / (mx - mn), 0.0f, 1.0f);
464:                 s_frac[idx] = Smooth(s_frac[idx], target, dt, dragging ? 40.0f : 14.0f);
465:                 const float fr = s_frac[idx];
466: 
467:                 // kart
468:                 Fill(row, Col(27, 30, 40, 255), S(10.0f));
469:                 if (dragging) Stroke(row, Accent(0.0f, 120), S(10.0f), S(1.2f));
470: 
471:                 Txt(S(17.0f), row.x + S(16.0f), row.y + S(8.0f), Col(214, 220, 232), label);
472:                 char buf[16];
473:                 snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.0f%s", *v, unit);
474:                 Txt(S(18.0f), row.x + row.w - S(16.0f) - TW(S(18.0f), buf), row.y + S(7.0f), Accent(0.0f), buf);
475: 
476:                 // - / + duymeleri
477:                 const float pm = Prs(id + 1, dt), pp = Prs(id + 2, dt);
478:                 Dot(minus.x + minus.w * 0.5f, cy, S(15.0f) - S(1.5f) * pm, Col(255, 255, 255, 14 + (int)(36.0f * pm)));
479:                 Dot(plus.x + plus.w * 0.5f,  cy, S(15.0f) - S(1.5f) * pp, Col(255, 255, 255, 14 + (int)(36.0f * pp)));
480:                 {
481:                         const float k = S(5.5f), mcx = minus.x + minus.w * 0.5f, pcx = plus.x + plus.w * 0.5f;
482:                         s_dl->AddLine(ImVec2(mcx - k, cy), ImVec2(mcx + k, cy), Col(226, 230, 240, 220), S(2.0f));
483:                         s_dl->AddLine(ImVec2(pcx - k, cy), ImVec2(pcx + k, cy), Col(226, 230, 240, 220), S(2.0f));
484:                         s_dl->AddLine(ImVec2(pcx, cy - k), ImVec2(pcx, cy + k), Col(226, 230, 240, 220), S(2.0f));
485:                 }
486: 
487:                 // yol (track)
488:                 const float th = S(6.0f), ty = cy - th * 0.5f;
489:                 Fill(Rc{ tx0, ty, tx1 - tx0, th }, Col(255, 255, 255, 22), S(3.0f));
490:                 const float kx = tx0 + (tx1 - tx0) * fr;
491:                 if (kx - tx0 > 1.0f) Fill(Rc{ tx0, ty, kx - tx0, th }, Accent(0.0f), S(3.0f));
492: 
493:                 // "standart (100%)" ish
494:                 if (mn < 100.0f && mx > 100.0f && unit[0] == '%') {
495:                         const float sx = tx0 + (tx1 - tx0) * ((100.0f - mn) / (mx - mn));
496:                         s_dl->AddLine(ImVec2(sx, ty - S(6.0f)), ImVec2(sx, ty + th + S(6.0f)), Col(255, 255, 255, 60), S(1.4f));
497:                 }
498: 
499:                 // topuz
500:                 const float kr = S(10.5f) + (dragging ? S(1.5f) : 0.0f);
501:                 Dot(kx, cy + S(1.0f), kr + S(1.5f), Col(0, 0, 0, 70));
502:                 Dot(kx, cy, kr, Col(245, 247, 252, 255));
503: 
504:                 return changed;
505:         }

528:         static void Arrow(float cx, float cy, float dir, ImU32 c)
529:         {
530:                 const float tip = cx + dir * S(18.0f), base = cx + dir * S(2.0f), tail = cx - dir * S(18.0f);

533:                 s_dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x0, cy - S(5.0f)), ImVec2(x1, cy + S(5.0f)), c);
534:         }

536:         // sinyal bloku: Sol / Avariya / Sag
537:         static void SignalBlock(const Rc& blk, uint8_t cur, CVehicleGTA* veh, float dt, bool canInput)
538:         {
539:                 s_blink = Smooth(s_blink, VehControls::BlinkOn() ? 1.0f : 0.0f, dt, 28.0f);
540: 
541:                 Fill(blk, Col(27, 30, 40, 255), S(10.0f));
542:                 Txt(S(15.0f), blk.x + S(18.0f), blk.y + S(10.0f), Col(124, 132, 152), "Sinyal");
543: 
544:                 const float gap = S(10.0f);
545:                 const float bw = (blk.w - S(32.0f) - gap * 2.0f) / 3.0f;
546:                 static const char* kNames[3] = { "Sol", "Avariya", "Sağ" };
547:                 static const uint8_t kModes[3] = { VEH_SIGNAL_LEFT, VEH_SIGNAL_HAZARD, VEH_SIGNAL_RIGHT };
548: 
549:                 for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k)
550:                 {
551:                         const Rc b{ blk.x + S(16.0f) + k * (bw + gap), blk.y + S(34.0f), bw, blk.h - S(50.0f) };
552:                         const int id = ID_SIG + k;
553:                         if (Tap(id, b, canInput) && veh) VehControls::ToggleSignal(veh, kModes[k]);
554: 
555:                         const float p = Prs(id, dt);
556:                         const Rc d = Shrink(b, S(1.5f) * p);
557:                         const bool on = (cur == kModes[k]);
558:                         const float glow = on ? (0.28f + 0.72f * s_blink) : 0.0f;
559: 
560:                         Fill(d, on ? Orange(30 + (int)(110.0f * glow)) : Col(36, 40, 52, 255), S(9.0f));
561:                         if (on) Stroke(d, Orange(120 + (int)(100.0f * glow)), S(9.0f), S(1.3f));
562: 
563:                         const ImU32 ic = on ? Col(255, 183, 84, 255) : Col(150, 158, 178, 255);
564:                         const float cx = d.x + d.w * 0.5f, cy = d.y + d.h * 0.38f;
565:                         if (k == 0)      Arrow(cx, cy, -1.0f, ic);
566:                         else if (k == 2) Arrow(cx, cy, +1.0f, ic);
567:                         else {
568:                                 s_dl->AddTriangle(ImVec2(cx, cy - S(14.0f)), ImVec2(cx - S(16.0f), cy + S(12.0f)), ImVec2(cx + S(16.0f), cy + S(12.0f)), ic, S(2.2f));
569:                                 s_dl->AddLine(ImVec2(cx, cy - S(5.0f)), ImVec2(cx, cy + S(2.0f)), ic, S(2.2f));

    18		this->addChild(m_input);
    19

    21		m_layoutLowerENG = new KeyboardLayoutLowerENG();
    22		this->addChild(m_layoutLowerENG);
    23		m_layoutLowerENG->setPadding(0.0f);
    24		m_layoutLowerENG->setSpacing(0.0f);
    25		m_layoutLowerENG->setVisible(false);
    26	
    27		m_layoutUpperENG = new KeyboardLayoutUpperENG();
    28		this->addChild(m_layoutUpperENG);
    29		m_layoutUpperENG->setPadding(0.0f);
    30		m_layoutUpperENG->setSpacing(0.0f);
    31		m_layoutUpperENG->setVisible(false);
    32	
    33		m_layoutLowerAZE = new KeyboardLayoutLowerAZE();
    34		this->addChild(m_layoutLowerAZE);
    35		m_layoutLowerAZE->setPadding(0.0f);
    36		m_layoutLowerAZE->setSpacing(0.0f);
    37		m_layoutLowerAZE->setVisible(false);
    38

    40		this->addChild(m_layoutUpperAZE);
    41		m_layoutUpperAZE->setPadding(0.0f);
    42		m_layoutUpperAZE->setSpacing(0.0f);

    58	
    59	void Keyboard::performLayout()
    60	{
    61		m_input->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    62		m_input->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));
    63	
    64		/* ENG */
    65		m_layoutLowerENG->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), height() - m_input->height()+50.0f));
    66		m_layoutLowerENG->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    67		m_layoutUpperENG->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), height() - m_input->height()+50.0f));
    68		m_layoutUpperENG->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    69	
    70		/* AZE */
    71		m_layoutLowerAZE->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), height() - m_input->height()+50.0f));
    72		m_layoutLowerAZE->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    73		m_layoutUpperAZE->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), height() - m_input->height()+50.0f));
    74		m_layoutUpperAZE->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    75	
    76		/* NUM */
    77		m_layoutNUM->setFixedSize(ImVec2(width(), height() - m_input->height()+50.0f));
    78		m_layoutNUM->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, UISettings::keyboardRowHeight()));
    79	
    80		Widget::performLayout();
    81	}
    82	
    83	void Keyboard::draw(ImGuiRenderer* renderer)
    84	{
    85		renderer->drawRect(absolutePosition(), absolutePosition() + size(), UISettings::keyboardBackgroundColor(), true);
    86		Widget::draw(renderer);
    87	}
    88	
    89	void Keyboard::setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type type)
    90	{
    91		m_activeLayout->setVisible(false);
    92	
    93		switch (type)
    94		{
    95			case KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_LOWER:
    96				m_layoutLowerENG->setVisible(true);
    97				m_activeLayout = m_layoutLowerENG;
    98				break;
    99	
   100			case KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_UPPER:
   101				m_layoutUpperENG->setVisible(true);
   102				m_activeLayout = m_layoutUpperENG;
   103				break;
   104	
   105			case KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_LOWER:
   106				m_layoutLowerAZE->setVisible(true);
   107				m_activeLayout = m_layoutLowerAZE;
   108				break;
   109	
   110			case KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_UPPER:
   111				m_layoutUpperAZE->setVisible(true);
   112				m_activeLayout = m_layoutUpperAZE;
   113				break;
   114	
   115			case KeyboardLayout::Type::NUM:
   116				m_layoutNUM->setVisible(true);
   117				m_activeLayout = m_layoutNUM;
   118				break;
   119		}
   120	
   121		m_activeLayoutType = type;
   122	}
   123	
   124	void Keyboard::changeCase(KeyboardLayout::KeyCase key_case)
   125	{
   126		switch (key_case)
   127		{
   128			case KeyboardLayout::KeyCase::LOWER:
   129				if (m_activeLayoutType == KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_UPPER) setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_LOWER);
   130				if (m_activeLayoutType == KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_UPPER) setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_LOWER);
   131				break;
   132	
   133			case KeyboardLayout::KeyCase::UPPER:
   134				if (m_activeLayoutType == KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_LOWER) setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_UPPER);
   135				if (m_activeLayoutType == KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_LOWER) setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_UPPER);
   136				break;
   137		}
   138	}
   139	
   140	void Keyboard::show(Widget* caller)
   141	{
   142		if(pSettings->Get().iAndroidKeyboard)
   143		{
   144			pJavaWrapper->ShowKeyboard();
   145		}
   146		else {
   147			m_history->reset();
   148			m_input->clear();
   149			this->setVisible(true);
   150			pGame->EnableGameInput(false);
   151		}
   152		m_caller = caller;
   153	}
   154	
   155	void Keyboard::hide()
   156	{
   157		if(pSettings->Get().iAndroidKeyboard)
   158		{
   159			pJavaWrapper->HideKeyboard();
   160		}
   161		else {
   162			this->setVisible(false);
   163			pGame->EnableGameInput(true);
   164		}
   165		m_caller = nullptr;
   166	}
   167	
   168	void Keyboard::send()
   169	{
   170		const std::string input = this->input()->inputString();
   171		m_history->add(input);
   172		if (m_caller) m_caller->keyboardEvent(input);
   173		this->hide();
   174	}
   175	
   176	void Keyboard::sendForGB(JNIEnv *pEnv, jobject thiz, jbyteArray str)
   177	{
   178		std::string::size_type v8;
   179		v8 = pEnv->functions->GetArrayLength(pEnv, (jarray)str);
   180		char* buffer = (char*)malloc(v8 + 1);
   181		jbyte* elements = pEnv->GetByteArrayElements(str, NULL);
   182		memcpy(buffer, elements, v8);
   183		buffer[v8] = 0;
   184		std::string input = std::string(buffer);
   185		if (m_caller) m_caller->keyboardEvent(input);
   186		hide();
   187		pEnv->ReleaseByteArrayElements(str, elements, JNI_ABORT);
   188	}
   189	
   190	void Keyboard::activateEvent(bool active)
   191	{
   192		if (active == false) this->hide();
   193	}
   194	
   195	/* KeyboardInput */
   196	KeyboardInput::KeyboardInput()
   197	{
   198		m_buttonTub = new Button("/", UISettings::fontSize());
   199		this->addChild(m_buttonTub);
   200		m_buttonPrev = new Button("<<", UISettings::fontSize());
   201		this->addChild(m_buttonPrev);
   202		m_buttonNext = new Button(">>", UISettings::fontSize());
   203		this->addChild(m_buttonNext);
   204	
   205		m_buttonPrev->setCallback([]()
   206								  {
   207									  std::string text = pUI->keyboard()->history()->prev();
   208									  pUI->keyboard()->input()->setInputString(text);
   209								  }
   210		);
   211	
   212		m_buttonNext->setCallback([]()
   213								  {
   214									  std::string text = pUI->keyboard()->history()->next();
   215									  pUI->keyboard()->input()->setInputString(text);
   216								  }
   217		);
   218	
   219		m_buttonTub->setCallback([]()
   220								 {
   221									 pUI->keyboard()->input()->setInputString("/");
   222								 }
   223		);
   224	
   225		m_buttonPrev->setVisible(true);
   226		m_buttonNext->setVisible(true);
   227		m_buttonTub->setVisible(true);
   228	}
   229	
   230	void KeyboardInput::performLayout()
   231	{
   232		Widget::performLayout();
   233	
   234		m_buttonTub->setPosition(
   235				ImVec2(width() - (m_buttonTub->width() + UISettings::padding() + m_buttonPrev->width()*2.3 + UISettings::padding()),
   236					   (height() - m_buttonTub->height()) / 2));
   237		m_buttonPrev->setPosition(
   238				ImVec2(width() - (m_buttonPrev->width() + UISettings::padding() + m_buttonNext->width() + UISettings::padding()),
   239					   (height() - m_buttonPrev->height()) / 2));
   240		m_buttonNext->setPosition(
   241				ImVec2(width() - (m_buttonNext->width() + UISettings::padding()),
   242					   (height() - m_buttonNext->height()) / 2));
   243	}
   244	
   245	void KeyboardInput::addCharToInput(char value)
   246	{
   247		m_input += value;
   248		m_caption = Encoding::cp2utf(m_input);
   249	}
   250	
   251	void KeyboardInput::addStringToInput(const std::string& value)
   252	{
   253		m_input += value;
   254		m_caption = Encoding::cp2utf(m_input);
   255	}
   256	
   257	void KeyboardInput::popCharFromInput()
   258	{
   259		if (m_input.empty()) return;
   260	
   261		// UTF-8 multibyte silinməsi üçün son simvolun uzunluğunu təyin edirik
   262		unsigned char c = m_input.back();
   263		int len = 1;
   264		if ((c & 0xE0) == 0xC0) len = 2;
   265		else if ((c & 0xF0) == 0xE0) len = 3;
   266		else if ((c & 0xF8) == 0xF0) len = 4;
   267	
   268		if (m_input.length() >= (size_t)len)
   269			m_input.erase(m_input.length() - len);
   270		else
   271			m_input.pop_back();
   272	
   273		m_caption = Encoding::cp2utf(m_input);
   274	}
   275	
   276	void KeyboardInput::draw(ImGuiRenderer* renderer)
   277	{
   278		float divLinePosY = (absolutePosition().y + size().y) - UISettings::outlineSize();
   279		float font_sz = UISettings::fontSize();
   280	
   281		renderer->drawText(absolutePosition() + ImVec2(UISettings::padding(), (height() - font_sz) / 2),
   282						   ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), m_caption, false, font_sz);
   283	
   284		renderer->drawLine(
   285				ImVec2(absolutePosition().x, divLinePosY),
   286				ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + size().x, divLinePosY),
   287				ImColor(0xF5, 0x91, 0x32), UISettings::outlineSize()
   288		);
   289	
   290		Widget::draw(renderer);
   291	}

   293	/* KeyboardLayout */
   294	
   295	KeyboardLayout::KeyboardLayout()
   296			: Layout(Orientation::VERTICAL)
   297	{
   298		for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
   299		{
   300			m_rows[i] = new Row();
   301			this->addChild(m_rows[i]);
   302		}
   303	}

   306	{
   307		Layout::performLayout();
   308	}

   316	}

   319	{

   322		return key;

   329		return key;

   339	/* KeyboardLayout::Row::Key */
   340	
   341	KeyboardLayout::Row::Key::Key(const std::string& caption, char value)
   342			: Button(caption)
   343	{
   344		m_extendedKey = ExtendedKey::NONE;
   345		m_value = value;
   346		m_isStringValue = false;
   347	}
   348	
   349	KeyboardLayout::Row::Key::Key(const std::string& caption, const std::string& valueStr)
   350			: Button(caption)
   351	{
   352		m_extendedKey = ExtendedKey::NONE;
   353		m_value = 0;
   354		m_valueStr = valueStr;
   355		m_isStringValue = true;
   356	}
   357	
   358	KeyboardLayout::Row::Key::Key(ExtendedKey extended_key)
   359			: Button("")
   360	{
   361		switch (extended_key)
   362		{
   363			case ExtendedKey::NUM:
   364				this->setCaption(std::string("123"));
   365				break;
   366			case ExtendedKey::LANG_ENG:
   367				this->setCaption(std::string("ENG"));
   368				break;
   369			case ExtendedKey::LANG_AZE:
   370				this->setCaption(std::string("AZE"));
   371				break;
   372		}
   373	
   374		m_extendedKey = extended_key;
   375	}
   376	
   377	void KeyboardLayout::Row::Key::draw(ImGuiRenderer* renderer)
   378	{
   379		if (focused()) renderer->drawRect(absolutePosition(), absolutePosition() + size(), ImColor(0xF5, 0x91, 0x32), true);
   380	
   381		switch (m_extendedKey)
   382		{
   383			case ExtendedKey::SHIFT_ON:
   384				renderer->drawTriangle(
   385						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.37f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f),
   386						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.50f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.25f),
   387						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.63f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f),
   388						ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), true
   389				);
   390				renderer->drawRect(
   391						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.44f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f - 1),
   392						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.56f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.68f),
   393						ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), true
   394				);
   395				break;
   396	
   397			case ExtendedKey::SHIFT_OFF:
   398				renderer->drawTriangle(
   399						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.37f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f),
   400						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.50f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.25f),
   401						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.63f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f),
   402						ImColor(0xF5, 0x91, 0x32), true
   403				);
   404				renderer->drawRect(
   405						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.44f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f - 1),
   406						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.56f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.68f),
   407						ImColor(0xF5, 0x91, 0x32), true
   408				);
   409				break;
   410	
   411			case ExtendedKey::BACKSPACE:
   412				static ImVec2 points[5];
   413				points[0] = ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.35f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f);
   414				points[1] = ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.45f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.25f);
   415				points[2] = ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.65f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.25f);
   416				points[3] = ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.65f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.65f);
   417				points[4] = ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.45f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.65f);
   418				renderer->drawConvexPolyFilled(points, 5, ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f));
   419				break;
   420	
   421			case ExtendedKey::SPACE:
   422				renderer->drawRect(
   423						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.07f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.35f),
   424						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.93f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.65f),
   425						ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), true
   426				);
   427				break;
   428	
   429			case ExtendedKey::SEND:
   430				renderer->drawTriangle(
   431						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.3f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.25f),
   432						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.3f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.75f),
   433						ImVec2(absolutePosition().x + width() * 0.7f, absolutePosition().y + height() * 0.50f),
   434						ImColor(1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f), true
   435				);
   436				break;
   437		}
   438	
   439		Widget::draw(renderer);
   440	}
   441	
   442	void KeyboardLayout::Row::Key::touchPopEvent()
   443	{
   444		Keyboard* keyboard = dynamic_cast<Keyboard*>(this->parent()->parent()->parent());
   445	
   446		switch (m_extendedKey)
   447		{
   448			case ExtendedKey::BACKSPACE:
   449				keyboard->input()->popCharFromInput();
   450				break;
   451	
   452			case ExtendedKey::SPACE:
   453				keyboard->input()->addCharToInput(' ');
   454				break;
   455	
   456			case ExtendedKey::SEND:
   457				keyboard->send();
   458				break;
   459	
   460			case ExtendedKey::SHIFT_ON:
   461				keyboard->changeCase(KeyCase::UPPER);
   462				break;
   463	
   464			case ExtendedKey::SHIFT_OFF:
   465				keyboard->changeCase(KeyCase::LOWER);
   466				break;
   467	
   468			case ExtendedKey::LANG_AZE:
   469				keyboard->setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::AZE_LOWER);
   470				break;
   471	
   472			case ExtendedKey::LANG_ENG:
   473				keyboard->setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::ENG_LOWER);
   474				break;
   475	
   476			case ExtendedKey::NUM:
   477				keyboard->setActiveLayout(KeyboardLayout::Type::NUM);
   478				break;
   479	
   480			case ExtendedKey::NONE:
   481				if (m_isStringValue)
   482					keyboard->input()->addStringToInput(m_valueStr);
   483				else
   484					keyboard->input()->addCharToInput(m_value);
   485				break;
   486		}
   487	}

   799	
   800	KeyboardHistory::KeyboardHistory()
   801	{
   802		m_index = 0;
   803		m_count = 0;
   804		m_vMessages.resize(KEYBOARD_MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES);
   805	}
   806	
   807	void KeyboardHistory::add(const std::string& input)
   808	{
   809		for (int i = KEYBOARD_MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES - 2; i > 0; i--)
   810		{
   811			std::string str = m_vMessages[i];
   812			m_vMessages[i] = m_vMessages[i - 1];
   813			m_vMessages[i - 1] = str;
   814		}
   815	
   816		m_vMessages[0] = input;
   817		if (m_count < KEYBOARD_MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES) m_count++;
   818	}
   819	
   820	void KeyboardHistory::reset()
   821	{
   822		m_index = 0;
   823	}
   824	
   825	const std::string KeyboardHistory::next()
   826	{
   827		if (m_count == 0) return "";
   828	
   829		std::string str = m_vMessages[m_index];
   830	
   831		if (m_index < m_count - 1) m_index++;
   832		return str;
   833	}
   834	
   835	const std::string KeyboardHistory::prev()
   836	{
   837		if (m_count == 0) return "";
   838	
   839		std::string str = m_vMessages[m_index];
   840		if (m_index > 0) m_index--;
   841		return str;
   842	}

    12	void InputWidget::performLayout()
    13	{
    14		float editBoxHeight = UISettings::dialogButtonPanelSize().y * 0.7f;
    15	
    16		/* ��������� ��������� */
    17		m_msgbox->setMinSize(ImVec2(
    18			this->minSize().x,
    19			this->minSize().y - editBoxHeight
    20		));
    21		m_msgbox->setMaxSize(ImVec2(
    22			this->maxSize().x,
    23			this->maxSize().y - editBoxHeight
    24		));
    25		m_msgbox->performLayout();
    26	
    27		ImVec2 dialog_size = ImVec2(m_msgbox->width(), m_msgbox->height() + editBoxHeight);
    28	
    29		this->setSize(dialog_size);
    30	
    31		/* ��������� ���� ����� */
    32		m_editbox->setSize(ImVec2(dialog_size.x, editBoxHeight));
    33		m_editbox->performLayout();
    34	
    35		/* ������������� �������  */
    36		m_msgbox->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, 0.0f));
    37		m_editbox->setPosition(ImVec2(0.0f, m_msgbox->position().y + m_msgbox->height()));
    38	}
    39

    41	{
    42		m_msgbox->setMessage(message);
    43	}

    24	void CPlayerTags::Render(ImGuiRenderer* renderer)
    25	{
    26		CVector vecPos;
    27		RwMatrix matLocal, matPlayer;
    28		int dwHitEntity;
    29		char szNickBuf[64];
    30	
    31	    static CCamera& TheCamera = *reinterpret_cast<CCamera*>(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x00951FA8 : 0xBBA8D0));
    32	
    33		if (pNetGame && pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bShowNameTags)
    34		{
    35			CPlayerPool* pPlayerPool = pNetGame->GetPlayerPool();
    36	        matLocal = pGame->FindPlayerPed()->m_pPed->GetMatrix().ToRwMatrix();
    37	
    38			for (PLAYERID playerId = 0; playerId < MAX_PLAYERS; playerId++)
    39			{
    40				if (pPlayerPool->GetSlotState(playerId) == true)
    41				{
    42					CRemotePlayer* pRemotePlayer = pPlayerPool->GetAt(playerId);
    43	
    44					if (pRemotePlayer && pRemotePlayer->IsActive() && pRemotePlayer->m_bShowNameTag && !pRemotePlayer->IsNPC())
    45					{
    46						CPlayerPed* pPlayerPed = pRemotePlayer->GetPlayerPed();
    47	
    48						if (pPlayerPed && pPlayerPed->m_pPed->GetDistanceFromCamera() <= pNetGame->m_pNetSet->fNameTagDrawDistance)
    49						{
    50							/*if (pRemotePlayer->GetState() == PLAYER_STATE_DRIVER &&
    51								pRemotePlayer->m_pCurrentVehicle &&
    52								pRemotePlayer->m_pCurrentVehicle->IsRCVehicle())
    53							{
    54								pRemotePlayer->m_pCurrentVehicle->GetMatrix(&matPlayer);
    55								vecPos.x = matPlayer.pos.x;
    56								vecPos.y = matPlayer.pos.y;
    57								vecPos.z = matPlayer.pos.z;
    58							}
    59							else
    60							{*/
    61								if (!pPlayerPed->m_pPed->IsAdded()) continue;
    62								vecPos.x = 0.0f;
    63								vecPos.y = 0.0f;
    64								vecPos.z = 0.0f;
    65								pPlayerPed->GetBonePosition(8, &vecPos);
    66							//}
    67	
    68							CAMERA_AIM* pCam = GameGetInternalAim();
    69							dwHitEntity = 0;
    70	
    71							if (pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bNameTagLOS)
    72							{
    73								dwHitEntity = CWorld::GetIsLineOfSightClear(vecPos, TheCamera.GetPosition(), true, false, false, true, false, false, false);
    74							}
    75	
    76							if (!pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bNameTagLOS || dwHitEntity && !pRemotePlayer->IsNPC())
    77							{
    78								sprintf(szNickBuf, "%s (%d)", pPlayerPool->GetPlayerName(playerId), playerId);
    79								this->Draw(renderer,&vecPos, szNickBuf,
    80									pRemotePlayer->GetPlayerColor(),
    81									pPlayerPed->m_pPed->GetDistanceFromCamera(),
    82									pRemotePlayer->m_fReportedHealth,
    83									pRemotePlayer->m_fReportedArmour,
    84									pRemotePlayer->m_bIsAFK,
    85									false);
    86							}
    87						}
    88					}
    89				}
    90			}
    91		}
    92	}

    94	// ===== AZFLAG_PATCH9 =====
    95	static void DrawAzFlag(ImDrawList* dl, ImVec2 p, float w, float h)
    96	{
    97		float s = h / 3.0f;
    98		dl->AddRectFilled(p, ImVec2(p.x + w, p.y + s), IM_COL32(0, 181, 226, 255));
    99		dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(p.x, p.y + s), ImVec2(p.x + w, p.y + 2 * s), IM_COL32(239, 51, 64, 255));
   100		dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(p.x, p.y + 2 * s), ImVec2(p.x + w, p.y + h), IM_COL32(80, 158, 47, 255));
   101		ImVec2 c(p.x + w * 0.44f, p.y + h * 0.5f);
   102		float r = h * 0.2f;
   103		dl->AddCircleFilled(c, r, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), 14);
   104		dl->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(c.x + r * 0.35f, c.y), r * 0.8f, IM_COL32(239, 51, 64, 255), 14);
   105		dl->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(c.x + r * 1.55f, c.y), r * 0.28f, IM_COL32(255, 255, 255, 255), 8);
   106		dl->AddRect(p, ImVec2(p.x + w, p.y + h), IM_COL32(0, 0, 0, 150));
   107	}
   108	
   109	void CPlayerTags::Draw(ImGuiRenderer* renderer, CVector* vec, const char* szNick, uint32_t dwColor, float fDist, float fHealth, float fArmour, bool bAfk, bool bMicro)
   110	{
   111		CVector vecTagPos;
   112	
   113		vecTagPos.x = vec->x;
   114		vecTagPos.y = vec->y;
   115		vecTagPos.z = vec->z;
   116		vecTagPos.z += 0.25f + (fDist * 0.0475f);
   117	
   118		CVector vecOut;
   119		// CSprite::CalcScreenCoors
   120		((void (*)(CVector*, CVector*, float*, float*, bool, bool))(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x005C57E8 + 1 : 0x6E9DF8)))(&vecTagPos, &vecOut, 0, 0, 0, 0);
   121	
   122		if (vecOut.z < 1.0f) return;
   123	
   124		// name (id)
   125		ImVec2 pos = ImVec2(vecOut.x, vecOut.y);
   126		//pos.x -= ImGui::CalcTextSize(szNick).x / 2;
   127		//ImGuiEx::AddOutlinedText(ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList(), pos, __builtin_bswap32(dwColor | (0x000000FF)), true, szNick);
   128		// AZFLAG_PATCH9: bayraq + ad, tund yarimseffaf panel icinde
   129		{
   130			const float fs = UISettings::fontSize() / 2;
   131			const ImVec2 ts = renderer->calculateTextSize(szNick, fs);
   132			const float flagH = fs * 0.95f;
   133			const float flagW = flagH * 1.5f;
   134			const float gap = fs * 0.35f;
   135			const float pad = fs * 0.3f;
   136			const float totalW = flagW + gap + ts.x;
   137			const float x0 = pos.x - totalW / 2;
   138	
   139			ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList();
   140			dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x0 - pad, pos.y - 1.0f), ImVec2(x0 + totalW + pad, pos.y + ts.y + 1.0f),
   141			                  IM_COL32(0, 0, 0, 110), fs * 0.4f);
   142			DrawAzFlag(dl, ImVec2(x0, pos.y + (ts.y - flagH) / 2), flagW, flagH);
   143			renderer->drawText(ImVec2(x0 + flagW + gap, pos.y), __builtin_bswap32(dwColor | (0x000000FF)),
   144			                   std::string(szNick), true, fs);
   145		}
   146	
   147	
   148		// Health bar
   149		if (fHealth < 0.0f) return;
   150		vecOut.x = (float)((int)vecOut.x);
   151		vecOut.y = (float)((int)vecOut.y);
   152	
   153		ImColor HealthBarBDRColor = ImColor(0x00, 0x64, 0x95, 0xED);
   154		ImColor HealthBarColor = ImColor(0xB9, 0x22, 0x28, 0xFF);
   155		ImColor HealthBarBGColor = ImColor(0x4B, 0x0B, 0x14, 0xFF);
   156	
   157		//float fWidth = (UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 3;
   158		//float fHeight = (UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 0.f;
   159		//float fOutline = 2.0f;
   160	
   161		float fWidth = UISettings::nametagBarSize().x;
   162		float fHeight = UISettings::nametagBarSize().y;
   163		float fOutline = UISettings::outlineSize();
   164	
   165	
   166		ImVec2 HealthBarBDR1;
   167		ImVec2 HealthBarBDR2;
   168		ImVec2 HealthBarBG1;
   169		ImVec2 HealthBarBG2;
   170		ImVec2 HealthBar1;
   171		ImVec2 HealthBar2;
   172	
   173		// top left
   174		HealthBarBDR1.x = vecOut.x - ((fWidth / 2) + fOutline);
   175		HealthBarBDR1.y = vecOut.y + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.2f);
   176		// bottom right
   177		HealthBarBDR2.x = vecOut.x + ((fWidth / 2) + fOutline);
   178		HealthBarBDR2.y = vecOut.y + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.2f) + fHeight;
   179	
   180		// top left
   181		HealthBarBG1.x = HealthBarBDR1.x + fOutline;
   182		HealthBarBG1.y = HealthBarBDR1.y + fOutline;
   183		// bottom right
   184		HealthBarBG2.x = HealthBarBDR2.x - fOutline;
   185		HealthBarBG2.y = HealthBarBDR2.y - fOutline;
   186		// top left
   187		HealthBar1.x = HealthBarBG1.x;
   188		HealthBar1.y = HealthBarBG1.y;
   189		// bottom right
   190		HealthBar2.y = HealthBarBG2.y;
   191	
   192		if (fHealth > 100.0f)
   193			fHealth = 100.0f;
   194	
   195		fHealth *= fWidth / 100.0f;
   196		fHealth -= (fWidth / 2);
   197		HealthBar2.x = vecOut.x + fHealth;
   198	
   199		float offsetY = 13.0f;//fHeight / 3;
   200	
   201		if (fArmour > 0.0f)
   202		{
   203			HealthBarBDR1.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   204			HealthBarBDR2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   205			HealthBarBG1.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   206			HealthBarBG2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   207			HealthBar1.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   208			HealthBar2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   209		}
   210	
   211		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBDR1, HealthBarBDR2, HealthBarBDRColor);
   212		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBG1, HealthBarBG2, HealthBarBGColor);
   213		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBar1, HealthBar2, HealthBarColor);
   214	
   215		// Armour Bar
   216		if (fArmour > 0.0f)
   217		{
   218			HealthBarBDR1.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   219			HealthBarBDR2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   220			HealthBarBG1.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   221			HealthBarBG2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   222			HealthBar1.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   223			HealthBar2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   224	
   225			HealthBarColor = ImColor(200, 200, 200, 255);
   226			HealthBarBGColor = ImColor(40, 40, 40, 255);
   227	
   228			if (fArmour > 100.0f)
   229				fArmour = 100.0f;
   230	
   231			fArmour *= fWidth / 100.0f;
   232			fArmour -= (fWidth / 2);
   233			HealthBar2.x = vecOut.x + fArmour;
   234	
   235			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBDR1, HealthBarBDR2, HealthBarBDRColor);
   236			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBG1, HealthBarBG2, HealthBarBGColor);
   237			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBar1, HealthBar2, HealthBarColor);
   238		}
   239	
   240		ImVec2 a = ImVec2(HealthBarBDR1.x - ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.4f), HealthBarBDR1.y);
   241		ImVec2 b = ImVec2(a.x + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f), a.y + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f));
   242	
   243		// micro icon
   244		if (bMicro)
   245		{
   246			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddImage((ImTextureID)m_pMicroIconTexture->raster, a, b);
   247		}
   248	
   249		// AFK icon
   250		if (bAfk)
   251		{
   252			ImVec2 a = ImVec2(HealthBarBDR1.x - ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.4f), HealthBarBDR1.y);
   253			ImVec2 b = ImVec2(a.x + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f), a.y + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f));
   254			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddImage((ImTextureID)m_pAFKIconTexture->raster, a, b);
   255		}
   256	}

    95	{

   118		if (fHealth < 0.0f) return;
   119		vecOut.x = (float)((int)vecOut.x);
   120		vecOut.y = (float)((int)vecOut.y);
   121	
   122		ImColor HealthBarBDRColor = ImColor(0x00, 0x64, 0x95, 0xED);
   123		ImColor HealthBarColor = ImColor(0xB9, 0x22, 0x28, 0xFF);
   124		ImColor HealthBarBGColor = ImColor(0x4B, 0x0B, 0x14, 0xFF);
   125

   128		//float fOutline = 2.0f;
   129	
   130		float fWidth = UISettings::nametagBarSize().x;
   131		float fHeight = UISettings::nametagBarSize().y;
   132		float fOutline = UISettings::outlineSize();

   136		ImVec2 HealthBarBDR2;

   151		HealthBarBG1.y = HealthBarBDR1.y + fOutline;

   154		HealthBarBG2.y = HealthBarBDR2.y - fOutline;

   157		HealthBar1.y = HealthBarBG1.y;
   158		// bottom right
   159		HealthBar2.y = HealthBarBG2.y;
   160

   162			fHealth = 100.0f;
   163	
   164		fHealth *= fWidth / 100.0f;
   165		fHealth -= (fWidth / 2);

   173			HealthBarBDR2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   174			HealthBarBG1.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   175			HealthBarBG2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   176			HealthBar1.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   177			HealthBar2.y += offsetY;//13.0f;
   178		}
   179	
   180		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBDR1, HealthBarBDR2, HealthBarBDRColor);
   181		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBarBG1, HealthBarBG2, HealthBarBGColor);
   182		ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBar1, HealthBar2, HealthBarColor);
   183	
   184		// Armour Bar
   185		if (fArmour > 0.0f)
   186		{
   187			HealthBarBDR1.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   188			HealthBarBDR2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   189			HealthBarBG1.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;
   190			HealthBarBG2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;

   192			HealthBar2.y -= offsetY;//13.0f;

   206			ImGui::GetBackgroundDrawList()->AddRectFilled(HealthBar1, HealthBar2, HealthBarColor);
   207		}
   208	
   209		ImVec2 a = ImVec2(HealthBarBDR1.x - ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.4f), HealthBarBDR1.y);
   210		ImVec2 b = ImVec2(a.x + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f), a.y + ((UISettings::fontSize() / 2) * 1.3f));

   213		if (bMicro)

   224		}

    26		CVector vecPos;

    28		int dwHitEntity;
    29		char szNickBuf[64];
    30

    32	
    33		if (pNetGame && pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bShowNameTags)

    37

    47	
    48						if (pPlayerPed && pPlayerPed->m_pPed->GetDistanceFromCamera() <= pNetGame->m_pNetSet->fNameTagDrawDistance)
    49						{
    50							/*if (pRemotePlayer->GetState() == PLAYER_STATE_DRIVER &&
    51								pRemotePlayer->m_pCurrentVehicle &&

    60							{*/
    61								if (!pPlayerPed->m_pPed->IsAdded()) continue;
    62								vecPos.x = 0.0f;

    64								vecPos.z = 0.0f;

    67	
    68							CAMERA_AIM* pCam = GameGetInternalAim();

    70	
    71							if (pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bNameTagLOS)
    72							{
    73								dwHitEntity = CWorld::GetIsLineOfSightClear(vecPos, TheCamera.GetPosition(), true, false, false, true, false, false, false);
    74							}
    75	
    76							if (!pNetGame->m_pNetSet->bNameTagLOS || dwHitEntity && !pRemotePlayer->IsNPC())
    77							{
    78								sprintf(szNickBuf, "%s (%d)", pPlayerPool->GetPlayerName(playerId), playerId);
    79								this->Draw(renderer,&vecPos, szNickBuf,

    81									pPlayerPed->m_pPed->GetDistanceFromCamera(),
    82									pRemotePlayer->m_fReportedHealth,
    83									pRemotePlayer->m_fReportedArmour,
    84									pRemotePlayer->m_bIsAFK,
    85									false);
    86							}
    87						}
    88					}
    89				}
    90			}
    91		}

    44	// 0.3.7
    45	unsigned char GetPacketID(Packet *p)
    46	{
    47	    if (p == 0) return 255;
    48	
    49	    if ((unsigned char)p->data[0] == ID_TIMESTAMP)
    50	        return (unsigned char)p->data[sizeof(unsigned char) + sizeof(unsigned long)];
    51	    else
    52	        return (unsigned char)p->data[0];
    53	}

   113	CNetGame::~CNetGame()
   114	{
   115	    m_pRakClient->Disconnect(0);
   116	    UnregisterRPCs(m_pRakClient);
   117	    UnregisterScriptRPCs(m_pRakClient);
   118	    RakNetworkFactory::DestroyRakClientInterface(m_pRakClient);
   119	
   120	    UninitializePools();
   121	
   122	    if (m_pNetSet) {

   124	        m_pNetSet = nullptr;
   125	    }
   126	}

   143	void CNetGame::UninitializePools()
   144	{

   146	
   147	    if (m_pPools->pPlayerPool) {
   148	        delete m_pPools->pPlayerPool;

   152	    if (m_pPools->pVehiclePool) {
   153	        delete m_pPools->pVehiclePool;
   154	        m_pPools->pVehiclePool = nullptr;
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    if (m_pPools->pGangZonePool) {
   158	        delete m_pPools->pGangZonePool;
   159	        m_pPools->pGangZonePool = nullptr;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    if (m_pPools->pPickupPool) {
   163	        delete m_pPools->pPickupPool;
   164	        m_pPools->pPickupPool = nullptr;
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    if (m_pPools->pObjectPool) {
   168	        delete m_pPools->pObjectPool;
   169	        m_pPools->pObjectPool = nullptr;
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    if (m_pPools->pTextLabelPool) {
   173	        delete m_pPools->pTextLabelPool;
   174	        m_pPools->pTextLabelPool = nullptr;
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    if (m_pPools->pTextDrawPool) {
   178	        delete m_pPools->pTextDrawPool;
   179	        m_pPools->pTextDrawPool = nullptr;
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    if (m_pPools->pActorPool) {
   183	        delete m_pPools->pActorPool;
   184	        m_pPools->pActorPool = nullptr;
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    if (m_pPools->pMenuPool) {

   189	        m_pPools->pMenuPool = nullptr;
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    if (m_pPools->pPlayerBubblePool) {
   193	        delete m_pPools->pPlayerBubblePool;
   194	        m_pPools->pPlayerBubblePool = nullptr;
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    delete m_pPools;
   198	    m_pPools = nullptr;
   199	}
   200	
   201	void CNetGame::Process()
   202	{
   203	    static uint32_t time = GetTickCount();
   204	
   205	    if (GetTickCount() - time >= 1000 / 30)
   206	    {
   207	        UpdateNetwork();
   208	        time = GetTickCount();
   209	    }
   210	
   211	    if (m_pNetSet->byteHoldTime) {
   212	        pGame->SetWorldTime(m_pNetSet->byteWorldTime_Hour, m_pNetSet->byteWorldTime_Minute);
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    if (GetGameState() == GAMESTATE_CONNECTED) {
   216	        ProcessPools();
   217	    }
   218	    else {
   219	        ProcessLoadingScreen();
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    if (GetGameState() == GAMESTATE_WAIT_CONNECT) {
   223	        ProcessConnecting();
   224	    }
   225	}
   226	
   227	void CNetGame::UpdateNetwork()
   228	{
   229	    Packet *pkt = nullptr;
   230	    unsigned char packetIdentifier;
   231	
   232	    while ((pkt = m_pRakClient->Receive()))
   233	    {
   234	        packetIdentifier = GetPacketID(pkt);
   235	
   236	        switch (packetIdentifier) {
   237	            case ID_AUTH_KEY:
   238	                Packet_AuthKey(pkt);
   239	                break;
   240	
   241	            case ID_CONNECTION_ATTEMPT_FAILED:
   242	                Packet_ConnectAttemptFailed(pkt);
   243	                break;
   244	
   245	            case ID_NO_FREE_INCOMING_CONNECTIONS:
   246	                Packet_NoFreeIncomingConnections(pkt);
   247	                break;
   248	
   249	            case ID_DISCONNECTION_NOTIFICATION:
   250	                Packet_DisconnectionNotification(pkt);
   251	                break;
   252	
   253	            case ID_CONNECTION_LOST:
   254	                Packet_ConnectionLost(pkt);
   255	                break;
   256	
   257	            case ID_CONNECTION_REQUEST_ACCEPTED:
   258	                Packet_ConnectionSucceeded(pkt);
   259	                break;
   260	
   261	            case ID_FAILED_INITIALIZE_ENCRIPTION:
   262	                Packet_FailedInitializeEncription(pkt);
   263	                break;
   264	
   265	            case ID_CONNECTION_BANNED:
   266	                Packet_ConnectionBanned(pkt);
   267	                SetGameState(GAMESTATE_WAIT_CONNECT);
   268	                break;
   269	
   270	            case ID_INVALID_PASSWORD:
   271	                Packet_InvalidPassword(pkt);
   272	                break;
   273	
   274	            case ID_VEHICLE_SYNC:
   275	                Packet_VehicleSync(pkt);
   276	                break;
   277	
   278	            case ID_AIM_SYNC:
   279	                Packet_AimSync(pkt);
   280	                break;
   281	
   282	            case ID_BULLET_SYNC:
   283	                Packet_BulletSync(pkt);
   284	                break;
   285	
   286	            case ID_PLAYER_SYNC:
   287	                Packet_PlayerSync(pkt);
   288	                break;
   289	
   290	            case ID_MARKERS_SYNC:
   291	                Packet_MarkerSync(pkt);
   292	                break;
   293	
   294	            case ID_UNOCCUPIED_SYNC:
   295	                Packet_UnoccupiedSync(pkt);
   296	                break;
   297	
   298	            case ID_TRAILER_SYNC:
   299	                Packet_TrailerSync(pkt);
   300	                break;
   301	
   302	            case ID_PASSENGER_SYNC:
   303	                Packet_PassengerSync(pkt);
   304	                break;
   305	
   306	            case Network::kRaknetPacketId: {
   307	                Network::OnRaknetReceive(pkt);
   308	                break;
   309	            }
   310	
   311	            case 251:
   312	                Packet_CustomRPC(pkt);
   313	                break;
   314	        }
   315	
   316	        m_pRakClient->DeallocatePacket(pkt);
   317	    }
   318	}
   319	
   320	void CNetGame::Packet_CustomRPC(Packet *p) {
   321	
   322	    RakNet::BitStream bs((unsigned char *) p->data, p->length, false);
   323	    bs.IgnoreBits(8);
   324	
   325	    uint32_t rpcID;
   326	    bs.Read(rpcID);
   327	
   328	    switch (rpcID) {
   329	        case 99: {
   330	            FLog("RPC_CHECK_CASH");
   331	            uint8_t bLen, bLen1;
   332	            uint16_t bVersion;
   333	            char szText[30];
   334	            char szText1[30];
   335	
   336	            memset(szText, 0, 30);
   337	            memset(szText1, 0, 30);
   338	
   339	            bs.Read(bLen);
   340	            if (bLen >= sizeof(szText) - 1)
   341	                return;
   342	
   343	            bs.Read(&szText[0], bLen);
   344	
   345	            bs.Read(bLen1);
   346	            if (bLen1 >= sizeof(szText1) - 1)
   347	                return;
   348	
   349	            bs.Read(&szText1[0], bLen1);
   350

    29	// ============================================================================
    30	//  FAR RENGI HOOK-U
    31	//
    32	//  Oyun on farin koronasini (CORONATYPE_HEADLIGHT) DoVehicleLights -> DoHeadLightEffect
    33	//  icinde RegisterCorona(eCoronaType) ile qeyde alir. Reng bayt-ofsetinde deyil,
    34	//  mehz bu cagirisin r/g/b parametrlerindedir. Buna gore burada hook qoyub, yalniz
    35	//  custom reng teyin olunmus masinin on far koronasinin rengini evez edirik.
    36	//
    37	//  Mehdudiyyet: yerdeki isiq konusu (DoHeadLightBeam) boyanmir, yalniz koronalar.
    38	// ============================================================================
    39	using RegisterCoronaTypeFn = void (*)(uint32, CEntityGTA*, uint8, uint8, uint8, uint8, const CVector*,
    40	                                      float, float, eCoronaType, eCoronaFlareType, bool, bool, int32,
    41	                                      float, bool, float, uint8, float, bool, bool);
    42	
    43	static RegisterCoronaTypeFn CCoronas__RegisterCorona_Type = nullptr;
    44	
    45	static void RegisterCorona_Type_hook(uint32 id, CEntityGTA* attachTo, uint8 red, uint8 green, uint8 blue, uint8 alpha,
    46	                                     const CVector* posn, float radius, float farClip, eCoronaType coronaType,
    47	                                     eCoronaFlareType flareType, bool enableReflection, bool checkObstacles,
    48	                                     int32 _param_not_used, float angle, bool longDistance, float nearClip,
    49	                                     uint8 fadeState, float fadeSpeed, bool onlyFromBelow, bool reflectionDelay)
    50	{
    51	    const auto& ho = CCoronas::s_HeadlightOverride;
    52	
    53	    if (ho.active && !ho.inTail
    54	        && (coronaType == eCoronaType::CORONATYPE_HEADLIGHT || coronaType == eCoronaType::CORONATYPE_HEADLIGHTLINE)
    55	        && attachTo != nullptr
    56	        && static_cast<const void*>(attachTo) == ho.vehicle)
    57	    {
    58	        red   = ho.r;
    59	        green = ho.g;
    60	        blue  = ho.b;
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    CCoronas__RegisterCorona_Type(id, attachTo, red, green, blue, alpha, posn, radius, farClip, coronaType,
    64	                                  flareType, enableReflection, checkObstacles, _param_not_used, angle,
    65	                                  longDistance, nearClip, fadeState, fadeSpeed, onlyFromBelow, reflectionDelay);
    66	}
    67	
    68	// Unvan ilə inline hook: CHook-da unvan overload-u varsa onu, yoxdursa shadowhook-u istifade edir.
    69	// (C++20 `requires` ile derleme zamani secilir -> hansi variant olursa olsun KOMPILYASIYA ugurlu olur.)
    70	template <typename H, typename O>
    71	static bool InstallInlineHook(uintptr_t addr, H hook, O* orig)
    72	{
    73	    if constexpr (requires { CHook::InlineHook(addr, hook, orig); }) {
    74	        CHook::InlineHook(addr, hook, orig);
    75	        return true;
    76	    } else {
    77	#ifdef CORONAS_HAVE_SHADOWHOOK
    78	        void* stub = shadowhook_hook_func_addr(reinterpret_cast<void*>(addr),
    79	                                               reinterpret_cast<void*>(hook),
    80	                                               reinterpret_cast<void**>(orig));
    81	        return stub != nullptr;
    82	#else
    83	        return false;
    84	#endif
    85	    }
    86	}
    87	
    88	// HEADLIGHT_RGB_PATCH ------------------------------------------------------
    89	namespace {
    90	    struct HlEntry {
    91	        uint32_t ct;
    92	        uint8 r, g, b;
    93	    };
    94	
    95	    std::unordered_map<const void*, HlEntry> g_hlColors;
    96	}
    97	
    98	void CCoronas::SetHeadlightRGB(
    99	    const void* veh,
   100	    uint8 r,
   101	    uint8 g,
   102	    uint8 b
   103	) {
   104	    if (!veh) return;
   105	
   106	    g_hlColors[veh] = HlEntry{
   107	        (uint32_t)static_cast<const CVehicleGTA*>(veh)->m_nCreationTime,
   108	        r, g, b
   109	    };
   110	}
   111	
   112	void CCoronas::ClearHeadlightRGB(const void* veh) {
   113	    g_hlColors.erase(veh);
   114	}
   115	
   116	bool CCoronas::GetHeadlightRGB(
   117	    const void* veh,
   118	    uint8& r,
   119	    uint8& g,
   120	    uint8& b
   121	) {
   122	    if (!veh || g_hlColors.empty())
   123	        return false;
   124	
   125	    auto it = g_hlColors.find(veh);
   126	
   127	    if (it == g_hlColors.end())
   128	        return false;
   129	
   130	    if (it->second.ct !=
   131	        (uint32_t)static_cast<const CVehicleGTA*>(veh)->m_nCreationTime) {
   132	
   133	        // masina yeniden yaranib
   134	        g_hlColors.erase(it);
   135	        return false;
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    r = it->second.r;
   139	    g = it->second.g;
   140	    b = it->second.b;
   141	
   142	    return true;
   143	}
   144	
   145	
   146	// Yerdeki far isigi: CShadows::StoreCarLightShadow.
   147	// Qirmizi (arxa) kolgelere toxunulmur.
   148	
   149	using StoreCarLightShadowFn = void (*)(
   150	    void*, int, RwTexture*, CVector*,
   151	    float, float, float, float,
   152	    uint8, uint8, uint8, float
   153	);
   154	
   155	static StoreCarLightShadowFn CShadows__StoreCarLightShadow = nullptr;
   156	
   157	
   158	static void StoreCarLightShadow_hook(
   159	    void* veh,
   160	    int id,
   161	    RwTexture* tex,
   162	    CVector* posn,
   163	    float a,
   164	    float b,
   165	    float c,
   166	    float d,
   167	    uint8 r,
   168	    uint8 g,
   169	    uint8 bl,
   170	    float maxViewAngle
   171	) {
   172	    const bool redDominant =
   173	        (r > g * 2 + 10) &&
   174	        (r > bl * 2 + 10);
   175	
   176	    if (veh && !redDominant) {
   177	        uint8 cr = 0, cg = 0, cb = 0;
   178	
   179	        bool have =
   180	            CCoronas::GetHeadlightRGB(veh, cr, cg, cb);
   181	
   182	        if (!have) {
   183	            const auto& ho = CCoronas::s_HeadlightOverride;
   184	
   185	            if (ho.active && !ho.inTail && ho.vehicle == veh) {
   186	                cr = ho.r;
   187	                cg = ho.g;
   188	                cb = ho.b;
   189	                have = true;
   190	            }
   191	        }
   192	
   193	        if (have) {
   194	            const int mx = std::max(
   195	                (int)r,
   196	                std::max((int)g, (int)bl)
   197	            );
   198	
   199	            const float k = (float)mx / 255.0f;
   200	
   201	            r  = (uint8)((float)cr * k);
   202	            g  = (uint8)((float)cg * k);
   203	            bl = (uint8)((float)cb * k);
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    CShadows__StoreCarLightShadow(
   208	        veh, id, tex, posn,
   209	        a, b, c, d,
   210	        r, g, bl,
   211	        maxViewAngle
   212	    );
   213	}
   214	
   215	
   216	void CCoronas::InjectHooks() {
   217	    CHook::Write(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x00679A84 : 0x851528), &CCoronas::SunScreenX);
   218	    CHook::Write(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x00679784 : 0x850F20), &CCoronas::SunScreenY);
   219	
   220	    CHook::Write(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x00679F40 : 0x851E90), &CCoronas::SunBlockedByClouds);
   221	    CHook::Write(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x00675F6C : 0x849F60), &CCoronas::MoonSize);
   222	
   223	    // Far rengi: RegisterCorona(eCoronaType) hook-u.
   224	    if (!InstallInlineHook(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x005A3A20 + 1 : 0x6C7174),
   225	                           &RegisterCorona_Type_hook, &CCoronas__RegisterCorona_Type)) {
   226	        FLog("CCoronas: RegisterCorona hook qurulmadi, far rengi islemeyecek");
   227	    }
   228	
   229	   // Yer isigi (kolge) rengi
   230	    CHook::InlineHook(
   231	        "_ZN8CShadows19StoreCarLightShadowEP8CVehicleiP9RwTextureP7CVectorffffhhhf",
   232	        &StoreCarLightShadow_hook,
   233	        &CShadows__StoreCarLightShadow
   234	    );
   235	
   236	   // CHook::InlineHook("_ZN8CCoronas4InitEv", &CCoronas::Init, &CCoronas__Init);
   237	}
   238	
   239	// Initialises coronas
   240	// 0x6FAA70
   241	
   242	void CCoronas::Init() {
   243	    {
   244	        CCoronas__Init();
   245	        FLog("CCoronas::Init()");
   246	
   247	        CTxdStore::ScopedTXDSlot txd{"particle"};
   248	        //for (auto&& [tex, name, maskName] : rng::zip_view{ gpCoronaTexture, aCoronastar, coronaTexturesAlphaMasks }) { // TODO: C++23
   249	        //    if (!tex) {
   250	        //        tex = RwTextureRead(name, maskName);
   251	        //    }
   252	        //}
   253	//        for (auto i = 0; i < CORONA_TEXTURES_COUNT; i++) {
   254	//           gpCoronaTexture[i] = CUtil::LoadTextureFromDB("txd", "coronamoon");
   255	//
   256	//        }
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    for (auto & corona : aCoronas) {
   260	        corona = CRegisteredCorona{}; // reset every corona to its default state
   261	    }
   262	}
   263	
   264	void CCoronas::Render() {
   265	    CHook::CallFunction<void>(g_libGTASA + (VER_x32 ? 0x005A23B8 + 1 : 0x6C5CD4) );
   266	}

---- FAYL: app/src/main/cpp/samp/game/Vehtuner.cpp  (248 sətir, ilk 600 göstərilir) ----
     1	// ============================================================================
     2	//  VehTuner.cpp  (YENI FAYL -> game/ qovluguna qoy, CMake-e elave et)
     3	//
     4	//  Niye kohne yanasma islemirdi:
     5	//   1) m_pHandlingData MODELE gore PAYLASILIR. Ora yazsan, eyni modelin
     6	//      butun masinlari (baska oyunculardaki de) deyisir. Burada hemin
     7	//      strukturun FERDI KOPYASI yaradilir ve yalniz bu masina baglanir.
     8	//   2) CPhysical::m_fMass masin yaranarkən handling-den bir defe kopyalanir.
     9	//      Yalniz handling->m_fMass-i deyismek fizikaya tesir etmir.
    10	//   3) Suret ucun yalniz m_fMaxVelocity kifayet deyil: viteslerin kecid
    11	//      cedveli (m_aGears) da miqyaslanmalidir.
    12	//   4) Sliderler indi "faiz"dir (100% = standart), buna gore oyunun daxili
    13	//      vahidlerini (fayl vahidi vs oyun vahidi) bilmeye ehtiyac yoxdur.
    14	// ============================================================================
    15	#include "VehTuner.h"
    16	#include "Entity/CVehicleGTA.h"
    17	
    18	#include <algorithm>
    19	#include <cmath>
    20	#include <mutex>
    21	#include <unordered_map>
    22	
    23	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24	// Sahe adlari tHandlingData.h ile TEYID OLUNUB (m_fMass, m_fBrakeDeceleration, m_fSteeringLock).
    25	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26	#define HND_MASS(h)   ((h)->m_fMass)
    27	#define HND_BRAKE(h)  ((h)->m_fBrakeDeceleration)
    28	#define HND_STEER(h)  ((h)->m_fSteeringLock)
    29	
    30	namespace
    31	{
    32	    // 0 = standart (override yoxdur, yalniz UI-da boz numune kimi gorunur)
    33	    constexpr uint8_t kPalette[VEHTUNER_COLOR_COUNT][3] = {
    34	        { 90,  90,  90},   //  0 standart
    35	        {255,  30,  30},   //  1 qirmizi
    36	        {255, 120,   0},   //  2 narinci
    37	        {255, 220,   0},   //  3 sari
    38	        {160, 255,   0},   //  4 lime
    39	        {  0, 255,  70},   //  5 yasil
    40	        {  0, 255, 180},   //  6 firuze-yasil
    41	        {  0, 230, 255},   //  7 cyan
    42	        {  0, 140, 255},   //  8 goy-mavi
    43	        { 40,  60, 255},   //  9 mavi
    44	        {130,  60, 255},   // 10 binovseyi
    45	        {200,   0, 255},   // 11 bənovseyi-qirmizi
    46	        {255,  60, 200},   // 12 çəhrayi
    47	        {255,   0, 100},   // 13 magenta
    48	        {170, 210, 255},   // 14 buz agi
    49	        {255, 200, 120},   // 15 isti ag
    50	    };
    51	
    52	    struct Entry
    53	    {
    54	        uint32_t       creationTime = 0;      // eyni adresde BASQA masini tanimaq ucun
    55	        tHandlingData* orig         = nullptr; // oyunun umumi handling-i (HECVAXT deyismirik)
    56	        tHandlingData* copy         = nullptr; // bu masina aid ferdi kopya
    57	        float          baseMass     = 0.0f;    // CPhysical::m_fMass-in ilkin deyeri
    58	        VehTune  tune;
    59	    };
    60	
    61	    std::unordered_map<CVehicleGTA*, Entry> g_entries;
    62	    std::mutex                              g_mutex;
    63	
    64	    inline float Clamp(float v, float lo, float hi) { return std::max(lo, std::min(hi, v)); }
    65	
    66	    inline bool Near100(float v) { return std::fabs(v - 100.0f) < 0.5f; }
    67	
    68	    inline bool IsNeutral(const VehTune& t)
    69	    {
    70	        return Near100(t.speedPct) && Near100(t.accelPct) && Near100(t.inertiaPct) && Near100(t.massPct) &&
    71	               Near100(t.dragPct) && Near100(t.brakePct) && Near100(t.steerPct) && Near100(t.tractionPct) &&
    72	               Near100(t.tracLossPct) && Near100(t.turnPct) && Near100(t.suspForcePct) &&
    73	               Near100(t.suspDampPct) && Near100(t.collisionPct);
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    // g_mutex artiq tutulub olmalidir.
    77	    Entry* Find(CVehicleGTA* veh)
    78	    {
    79	        auto it = g_entries.find(veh);
    80	        if (it == g_entries.end()) return nullptr;
    81	
    82	        if (it->second.creationTime != veh->m_nCreationTime)
    83	        {
    84	            // Bu adresde artiq basqa masin var (kohne masin silinib).
    85	            // Yeni masin oyunun oz handling-i ile yaranib, bizim kopyaya baxmir.
    86	            delete it->second.copy;
    87	            g_entries.erase(it);
    88	            return nullptr;
    89	        }
    90	        return &it->second;
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    Entry& FindOrCreate(CVehicleGTA* veh)
    94	    {
    95	        if (Entry* e = Find(veh)) return *e;
    96	        Entry e;
    97	        e.creationTime = veh->m_nCreationTime;
    98	        return g_entries.emplace(veh, e).first->second;
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    void RestoreHandling(CVehicleGTA* veh, Entry& e)
   102	    {
   103	        if (!e.copy) return;
   104	        if (veh->m_pHandlingData == e.copy) veh->m_pHandlingData = e.orig;   // ƏVVƏL pointeri qaytar
   105	        veh->m_fMass = e.baseMass;
   106	        delete e.copy;                                                       // SONRA sil
   107	        e.copy = nullptr;
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    void ApplyHandling(CVehicleGTA* veh, Entry& e)
   111	    {
   112	        VehTune& t = e.tune;
   113	        t.speedPct    = Clamp(t.speedPct,    50.0f, 250.0f);
   114	        t.accelPct    = Clamp(t.accelPct,    30.0f, 400.0f);
   115	        t.inertiaPct  = Clamp(t.inertiaPct,  20.0f, 300.0f);
   116	        t.massPct     = Clamp(t.massPct,     30.0f, 300.0f);
   117	        t.dragPct     = Clamp(t.dragPct,     20.0f, 300.0f);
   118	        t.brakePct    = Clamp(t.brakePct,    30.0f, 300.0f);
   119	        t.steerPct    = Clamp(t.steerPct,    50.0f, 150.0f);
   120	        t.tractionPct = Clamp(t.tractionPct, 50.0f, 200.0f);
   121	        t.tracLossPct = Clamp(t.tracLossPct, 20.0f, 200.0f);
   122	        t.turnPct     = Clamp(t.turnPct,     30.0f, 300.0f);
   123	        t.suspForcePct= Clamp(t.suspForcePct,30.0f, 300.0f);
   124	        t.suspDampPct = Clamp(t.suspDampPct, 30.0f, 300.0f);
   125	        t.collisionPct= Clamp(t.collisionPct,10.0f, 300.0f);
   126	        if (t.headlight >= VEHTUNER_COLOR_COUNT) t.headlight = 0;
   127	
   128	        if (IsNeutral(t))
   129	        {
   130	            RestoreHandling(veh, e);
   131	            return;
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        if (!e.copy)
   135	        {
   136	            if (!veh->m_pHandlingData) return;
   137	            e.orig     = veh->m_pHandlingData;
   138	            e.baseMass = veh->m_fMass;
   139	            e.copy     = new tHandlingData(*e.orig);
   140	        }
   141	        veh->m_pHandlingData = e.copy;      // ferdi kopyaya bagla
   142	
   143	        const float kSpd = t.speedPct * 0.01f;
   144	        const float kAcc = t.accelPct * 0.01f;
   145	        const float kIne = t.inertiaPct * 0.01f;
   146	        const float kMas = t.massPct  * 0.01f;
   147	        const float kDrg = t.dragPct  * 0.01f;
   148	        const float kBrk = t.brakePct * 0.01f;
   149	        const float kStr = t.steerPct * 0.01f;
   150	        const float kTrc = t.tractionPct * 0.01f;
   151	        const float kTrl = t.tracLossPct * 0.01f;
   152	        const float kTrn = t.turnPct  * 0.01f;
   153	
   154	        // Her zaman ORIGINAL-dan hesablayiriq -> tekrar tetbiq edende yigilmir (idempotent).
   155	        const cTransmission& to = e.orig->m_transmissionData;
   156	        cTransmission&       tc = e.copy->m_transmissionData;
   157	
   158	        tc.m_fMaxVelocity           = to.m_fMaxVelocity        * kSpd;
   159	        tc.m_fMaxGearVelocity       = to.m_fMaxGearVelocity    * kSpd;
   160	        tc.m_maxReverseGearVelocity = to.m_maxReverseGearVelocity;
   161	        tc.m_fEngineAcceleration    = to.m_fEngineAcceleration * kAcc;
   162	        tc.m_fEngineInertia         = to.m_fEngineInertia      * kIne;
   163	
   164	        // Vites cedveli: her tTransmissionGear-in butun sahələri suretdir -> hamisini miqyasla.
   165	        static_assert(sizeof(tTransmissionGear) % sizeof(float) == 0, "tTransmissionGear float deyil");
   166	        constexpr size_t kFloatsPerGear = sizeof(tTransmissionGear) / sizeof(float);
   167	        for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i)
   168	        {
   169	            const float* src = reinterpret_cast<const float*>(&to.m_aGears[i]);
   170	            float*       dst = reinterpret_cast<float*>(&tc.m_aGears[i]);
   171	            for (size_t j = 0; j < kFloatsPerGear; ++j) dst[j] = src[j] * kSpd;
   172	        }
   173	
   174	        HND_BRAKE(e.copy) = HND_BRAKE(e.orig) * kBrk;
   175	        HND_STEER(e.copy) = HND_STEER(e.orig) * kStr;
   176	        HND_MASS(e.copy)  = HND_MASS(e.orig)  * kMas;
   177	        e.copy->m_fMassRecpr = e.orig->m_fMassRecpr / kMas;            // 1/kutle ayrica saxlanir
   178	        e.copy->m_fTurnMass  = e.orig->m_fTurnMass * kMas * kTrn;      // kutle ile birge + oz slideri
   179	        e.copy->m_fDragMult  = e.orig->m_fDragMult * kDrg;
   180	
   181	        e.copy->m_fTractionMultiplier = e.orig->m_fTractionMultiplier * kTrc;
   182	        e.copy->m_fTractionLoss       = e.orig->m_fTractionLoss       * kTrl;
   183	
   184	        e.copy->m_fSuspensionForceLevel   = e.orig->m_fSuspensionForceLevel   * (t.suspForcePct * 0.01f);
   185	        e.copy->m_fSuspensionDampingLevel = e.orig->m_fSuspensionDampingLevel * (t.suspDampPct  * 0.01f);
   186	        e.copy->m_fCollisionDamageMultiplier = e.orig->m_fCollisionDamageMultiplier * (t.collisionPct * 0.01f);
   187	
   188	        // Fizika kutlesi handling-den AYRI saxlanir -> onu da yenile.
   189	        veh->m_fMass = e.baseMass * kMas;
   190	    }
   191	}
   192	
   193	namespace VehTuner
   194	{
   195	    VehTune Get(CVehicleGTA* veh)
   196	    {
   197	        if (!veh) return VehTune{};
   198	        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(g_mutex);
   199	        if (Entry* e = Find(veh)) return e->tune;
   200	        return VehTune{};
   201	    }
   202	
   203	    void Set(CVehicleGTA* veh, const VehTune& t)
   204	    {
   205	        if (!veh) return;
   206	        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(g_mutex);
   207	        Entry& e = FindOrCreate(veh);
   208	        e.tune = t;
   209	        ApplyHandling(veh, e);
   210	    }
   211	
   212	    void Reset(CVehicleGTA* veh)
   213	    {
   214	        if (!veh) return;
   215	        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(g_mutex);
   216	        if (Entry* e = Find(veh))
   217	        {
   218	            RestoreHandling(veh, *e);
   219	            g_entries.erase(veh);
   220	        }
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    bool GetHeadlightRGB(CVehicleGTA* veh, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b)
   224	    {
   225	        if (!veh) return false;
   226	        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(g_mutex);
   227	        if (g_entries.empty()) return false;
   228	
   229	        Entry* e = Find(veh);
   230	        if (!e) return false;
   231	
   232	        const uint8_t id = e->tune.headlight;
   233	        if (id == 0 || id >= VEHTUNER_COLOR_COUNT) return false;
   234	
   235	        r = kPalette[id][0];
   236	        g = kPalette[id][1];
   237	        b = kPalette[id][2];
   238	        return true;
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    void GetPaletteColor(int id, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b)
   242	    {
   243	        if (id < 0 || id >= VEHTUNER_COLOR_COUNT) id = 0;
   244	        r = kPalette[id][0];
   245	        g = kPalette[id][1];
   246	        b = kPalette[id][2];
   247	    }
   248	}

---- FAYL: app/src/main/cpp/samp/game/Vehtuner.h  (55 sətir, ilk 600 göstərilir) ----
     1	#pragma once
     2	// ============================================================================
     3	//  VehTuner.h  (YENI FAYL -> game/ qovluguna qoy)
     4	//
     5	//  Ferdi (yalniz bir masina aid) handling + far rengi sistemi.
     6	//  Kohne CVehicle::SetMaxSpeed/SetHeadlightColor ... funksiyalari artiq
     7	//  ISTIFADE OLUNMUR (silmek olar, amma silmek mecburi deyil).
     8	// ============================================================================
     9	#include <cstdint>
    10	
    11	struct CVehicleGTA;
    12	
    13	// Butun handling deyerleri "faiz"dir: 100 = standart.
    14	// Bu, oyunun daxili vahidlerinden asili olmadan duzgun isleyir.
    15	struct VehTune
    16	{
    17	    // --- Muherrik ---
    18	    float   speedPct    = 100.0f;   // maks. suret                    (50 .. 250)
    19	    float   accelPct    = 100.0f;   // tecil = yerinden goturme       (30 .. 400)
    20	    float   inertiaPct  = 100.0f;   // muherrik ataleti (az = tez)    (20 .. 300)
    21	    float   massPct     = 100.0f;   // kutle                          (30 .. 300)
    22	    float   dragPct     = 100.0f;   // hava muqavimeti                (20 .. 300)
    23	    // --- Idareetme ---
    24	    float   brakePct    = 100.0f;   // tormoz                         (30 .. 300)
    25	    float   steerPct    = 100.0f;   // sukan bucagi                   (50 .. 150)
    26	    float   tractionPct = 100.0f;   // yapisma (traction)             (50 .. 200)
    27	    float   tracLossPct = 100.0f;   // sürüsme itkisi (traction loss) (20 .. 200)
    28	    float   turnPct     = 100.0f;   // firlanma ataleti (kutleye elave)(30 .. 300)
    29	    // --- Asma / zerbe ---
    30	    float   suspForcePct = 100.0f;  // asma gucu                      (30 .. 300)
    31	    float   suspDampPct  = 100.0f;  // asma sonduruculeri             (30 .. 300)
    32	    float   collisionPct = 100.0f;  // toqquşma zererı                (10 .. 300)
    33	    // --- Far ---
    34	    uint8_t headlight   = 0;        // 0 = standart, 1..15 = palitra rengi
    35	};
    36	
    37	constexpr int VEHTUNER_COLOR_COUNT = 16;
    38	
    39	namespace VehTuner
    40	{
    41	    // Masinin hazirki tenzimlemesi (yoxdursa standart).
    42	    VehTune Get(CVehicleGTA* veh);
    43	
    44	    // Canli tetbiq edir (hem handling, hem far rengi). Tekrar-tekrar cagirmaq tehlukesizdir.
    45	    void Set(CVehicleGTA* veh, const VehTune& t);
    46	
    47	    // Hamisini standarta qaytarir.
    48	    void Reset(CVehicleGTA* veh);
    49	
    50	    // Far rengi (DoVehicleLights hook-u istifade edir). Override yoxdursa false.
    51	    bool GetHeadlightRGB(CVehicleGTA* veh, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b);
    52	
    53	    // UI ucun palitra rengi (0 = "standart" ucun boz numune).
    54	    void GetPaletteColor(int id, uint8_t& r, uint8_t& g, uint8_t& b);
    55	}

    66				return m_pVehicles[vehicleID];
    67	
    68			return nullptr;
    69		}

    72		{
    73			if (VehicleID < MAX_VEHICLES)
    74				return m_bVehicleSlotState[VehicleID];
    75	
    76			return false;
    77		}

    79		VEHICLEID FindIDFromGtaPtr(CVehicleGTA* pGtaVehicle)
    80		{
    81			for (int i = 0; i < MAX_VEHICLES; i++)
    82			{
    83				if (m_pGTAVehicles[i] == pGtaVehicle) {
    84					return i;
    85				}
    86			}
    87		}
    88	
    89		// 0.3.7
    90		uint32_t FindGtaIDFromID(VEHICLEID VehicleID)
    91		{
    92			if (m_pGTAVehicles[VehicleID]) {
    93				return GamePool_Vehicle_GetIndex(m_pGTAVehicles[VehicleID]);
    94			}
    95	        else
    96	            return INVALID_VEHICLE_ID;
    97		}

    70	    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String[]> hndPicker = registerForActivityResult(
    71	            new ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument(), new ActivityResultCallback<Uri>() {
    72	                @Override
    73	                public void onActivityResult(Uri uri) {
    74	                    if (uri != null) copyHandling(uri);
    75	                }
    76	            });
    77	
    78	    private File hndTarget() {
    79	        File dir = new File(requireActivity().getExternalFilesDir(null), "SAMP");
    80	        if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs();
    81	        return new File(dir, "handling_custom.cfg");
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    private void copyHandling(Uri uri) {
    85	        try {
    86	            InputStream in = requireActivity().getContentResolver().openInputStream(uri);
    87	            if (in == null) throw new IOException("fayl açıla bilmədi");
    88	            FileOutputStream os = new FileOutputStream(hndTarget());
    89	            byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
    90	            int n;
    91	            long total = 0;
    92	            while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) { os.write(buf, 0, n); total += n; }
    93	            os.close();
    94	            in.close();
    95	            Toast.makeText(getActivity(), "handling.cfg yükləndi (" + total + " bayt). Oyunu yenidən başlat.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
    96	        } catch (Exception e) {
    97	            Toast.makeText(getActivity(), "Xəta: " + e.getMessage(), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
    98	        }
    99	    }
   100	
   101

   117	        mFPSSeekBar = view.findViewById(R.id.fps_seekbar);
   118	        mFPSText = view.findViewById(R.id.fps_count);
   119

   121	        Button hndPick = view.findViewById(R.id.handling_pick);
   122	        Button hndReset = view.findViewById(R.id.handling_reset);
   123	        hndPick.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
   124	            @Override
   125	            public void onClick(View v) {
   126	                hndPicker.launch(new String[]{"*/*"});
   127	            }
   128	        });
   129	        hndReset.setOnClickListener(new View.OnClickListener() {
   130	            @Override
   131	            public void onClick(View v) {
   132	                boolean ok = hndTarget().delete();

   358	    @Override
   359	    public void onResume() {
   360	        super.onResume();
   361	
   362	        mKeyboardSwitch.setChecked(new SharedPreferenceCore().getBoolean(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "ANDROID_KEYBOARD"));
   363	        mVoiceSwitch.setChecked(new SharedPreferenceCore().getBoolean(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "AIM"));
   364	        mFPSSwitch.setChecked(new SharedPreferenceCore().getBoolean(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "FPS_DISPLAY"));
   365	        mModifySwitch.setChecked(new SharedPreferenceCore().getBoolean(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "MODIFIED_DATA"));
   366	        mMonetSwitch.setChecked(new SharedPreferenceCore().getBoolean(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "MLOADER"));
   367	        autoCompleteTextView.setSelection(new SharedPreferenceCore().getInt(requireContext().getApplicationContext(), "VERSION"));
   368	
   369	        int fps = new SharedPreferenceCore().getInt(getContext(), "FPS_LIMIT");
   370	        switch (fps)
   371	        {
   372	            case 30: mFPSSeekBar.setProgress(0); break;
   373	            case 60: mFPSSeekBar.setProgress(1); break;
   374	            case 90: mFPSSeekBar.setProgress(2); break;
   375	            case 120: mFPSSeekBar.setProgress(3); break;
   376	        }
   377	        mFPSText.setText(String.valueOf(fps));
   378	
   379	        int message = new SharedPreferenceCore().getInt(getContext(), "MESSAGE_COUNT");
   380	        switch (message)
   381	        {
   382	            case 6: mMessagesSeekBar.setProgress(0); break;
   383	            case 9: mMessagesSeekBar.setProgress(1); break;
   384	            case 12: mMessagesSeekBar.setProgress(2); break;
   385	            case 15: mMessagesSeekBar.setProgress(3); break;
   386	        }
   387	        mMessagesText.setText(String.valueOf(message));
   388	    }
